"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">得 — trois usages à distinguer</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>得(de)</b> complément de degré : V+得+adv. <b>得(děi)</b> devoir (obligation). <b>得(dé)</b> obtenir.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 她跑得很快。(de = manière)<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">pǎo</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t4"">kuài</span>。(de=manière)</small><br><i>Elle court très vite.</i><br><br>• 你得先完成作业。(děi = obligation)<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">děi</span> <span class=""t1"">xiān</span> <span class=""t2"">wán</span><span class=""t2"">chéng</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span><span class=""t4"">yè</span>。(děi=obligation)</small><br><i>Tu dois d'abord finir tes devoirs.</i><br><br>• 他得到了奖励。(dé = obtenir)<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">dé</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t3"">jiǎng</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>。(dé=obtenir)</small><br><i>Il a obtenu une récompense.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">让/叫/使/令 — causatif</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Tous signifient ""faire faire"" ou ""rendre + adj"", mais avec des nuances :<br><b>让</b> = permettre/laisser · <b>叫</b> = ordonner/faire (oral) · <b>使</b> = causer (soutenu/abstrait) · <b>令</b> = rendre (registre formel/littéraire).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 妈妈让我去买菜。<br><small><span class=""t1"">mā</span><span class=""t0"">ma</span> <span class=""t4"">ràng</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t3"">mǎi</span><span class=""t4"">cài</span>。</small><br><i>Maman m'a demandé d'aller faire les courses.</i><br><br>• 这首歌让他很感动。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t3"">shǒu</span><span class=""t1"">gē</span> <span class=""t4"">ràng</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t3"">gǎn</span><span class=""t4"">dòng</span>。</small><br><i>Cette chanson l'a beaucoup touché.</i><br><br>• 这个消息令人震惊。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t1"">xiāo</span><span class=""t0"">xi</span> <span class=""t4"">lìng</span><span class=""t2"">rén</span><span class=""t4"">zhèn</span><span class=""t1"">jīng</span>。</small><br><i>Cette nouvelle est stupéfiante.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">差不多 / 几乎 / 大约 — approximatif</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>差不多</b> = à peu près (similaire à). <b>几乎</b> = presque (très proche de). <b>大约</b> = environ (quantité).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 差不多两个小时到。<br><small><span class=""t4"">chà</span><span class=""t0"">bu</span><span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t3"">liǎng</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎo</span><span class=""t2"">shí</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span>。</small><br><i>Ça prend à peu près deux heures.</i><br><br>• 他几乎每天都来。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">jī</span><span class=""t1"">hū</span> <span class=""t3"">měi</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span>。</small><br><i>Il vient presque tous les jours.</i><br><br>• 大约三百人参加了会议。<br><small><span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t1"">yuē</span> <span class=""t1"">sān</span><span class=""t3"">bǎi</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t1"">cān</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span>。</small><br><i>Environ trois cents personnes ont assisté à la réunion.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">到底 — au fond / en fin de compte / diable</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>到底</b> dans les questions = expression d'impatience ou de vouloir une réponse définitive.<br>Dans les affirmations = finalement / au bout du compte (résultat après une longue attente).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 你到底想要什么？<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t3"">dǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span>？</small><br><i>Qu'est-ce que tu veux au fond ?</i><br><br>• 他到底来了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t3"">dǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Il est finalement venu.</i><br><br>• 这到底是怎么回事？<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t3"">dǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">zěn</span><span class=""t0"">me</span><span class=""t2"">huí</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span>？</small><br><i>Mais qu'est-ce qui se passe au juste ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">差点(儿) — avoir failli</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>差点</b> + V = avoir failli V (sous-entendu : ça ne s'est pas produit, ouf). <b>差点没</b> + V : si V est redouté, même sens que 差点 + V (差点没摔倒 = a failli tomber) ; si V est souhaité, = a failli ne pas V, mais V a bien eu lieu (差点没赶上 = a failli le rater).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我差点忘了带钥匙。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t4"">chà</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">wàng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">dài</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span><span class=""t0"">shi</span>。</small><br><i>J'ai failli oublier de prendre mes clés.</i><br><br>• 她差点哭出来了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">chà</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t1"">kū</span> <span class=""t1"">chū</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Elle a failli fondre en larmes.</i><br><br>• 车差点撞上了行人。<br><small><span class=""t1"">chē</span> <span class=""t4"">chà</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">zhuàng</span> <span class=""t4"">shàng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t2"">xíng</span><span class=""t2"">rén</span>。</small><br><i>La voiture a failli renverser un piéton.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">再说 — en plus / de toute façon / on verra</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>再说</b> a deux usages :<br>1. De plus / en outre (connecteur). <br>2. On verra le moment venu / à plus tard (reporter une décision).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这事太难了，再说我也没时间。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t2"">nán</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t1"">jiān</span>。</small><br><i>C'est trop difficile, et de toute façon je n'ai pas le temps.</i><br><br>• 先把眼前的事做完再说吧。<br><small><span class=""t1"">xiān</span> <span class=""t3"">bǎ</span> <span class=""t3"">yǎn</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t2"">wán</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t0"">ba</span>。</small><br><i>Finissons d'abord ce qui est devant nous, on verra ensuite.</i><br><br>• 去不去以后再说，现在不必决定。<br><small><span class=""t4"">qù</span><span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t4"">hòu</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t4"">xiàn</span><span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">bì</span> <span class=""t2"">jué</span><span class=""t4"">dìng</span>。</small><br><i>Aller ou non, on verra plus tard — pas besoin de décider maintenant.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">连 + A + 都/也 + B — même A fait B</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	<b>Carte en double.</b><br><br>Le même point est traité par la fiche « 连…都/也… — même…    HSK 3 ». Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">看来 / 看样子 — il semble que / on dirait que</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>看来</b> / <b>看样子</b> = il semble que, à voir les signes. Exprime une déduction à partir d'indices observés.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 看来他今天不打算来了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">kàn</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">jīn</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t3"">dǎ</span><span class=""t0"">suan</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Il semble qu'il n'ait pas l'intention de venir aujourd'hui.</i><br><br>• 看样子要下雨了，带把伞吧。<br><small><span class=""t4"">kàn</span><span class=""t4"">yàng</span><span class=""t0"">zi</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t4"">xià</span><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t4"">dài</span> <span class=""t3"">bǎ</span> <span class=""t3"">sǎn</span> <span class=""t0"">ba</span>。</small><br><i>On dirait qu'il va pleuvoir, prenons un parapluie.</i><br><br>• 看来这件事比想象的复杂多了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">kàn</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t3"">bǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span><span class=""t4"">xiàng</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">fù</span><span class=""t2"">zá</span> <span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Il semble que cette affaire soit bien plus compliquée qu'on ne le pensait.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">把 + 宾语 + V + 结果补语 — résultats complexes</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	<b>Carte en double.</b><br><br>Le même point est traité par la fiche « 把 — objets directs longs / résultats complexes    HSK 3 ». Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">被动句无施事 — passif sans agent</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>La structure 被 peut s'utiliser sans mentionner l'agent quand il est inconnu, évident ou non pertinent.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我的手机被偷了。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">shǒu</span><span class=""t1"">jī</span> <span class=""t4"">bèi</span> <span class=""t1"">tōu</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Mon téléphone a été volé (par quelqu'un).</i><br><br>• 这件事已经被处理了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t1"">jīng</span> <span class=""t4"">bèi</span> <span class=""t3"">chǔ</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Cette affaire a déjà été traitée.</i><br><br>• 错误被发现了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">cuò</span><span class=""t4"">wù</span> <span class=""t4"">bèi</span> <span class=""t1"">fā</span><span class=""t4"">xiàn</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>L'erreur a été découverte.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">V + 起来 — commencer à / semble… (complément directionnel figuratif)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>V + 起来</b> peut signifier :<br>1. Commencer à (action qui débute). <br>2. Paraître / sembler (avec les sens, évaluation).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 她突然哭起来了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">tū</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t1"">kū</span> <span class=""t3"">qǐ</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Elle s'est soudainement mise à pleurer.</i><br><br>• 这道菜闻起来很香。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t4"">cài</span> <span class=""t2"">wén</span> <span class=""t3"">qǐ</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t1"">xiāng</span>。</small><br><i>Ce plat sent bon.</i><br><br>• 看起来他不太高兴。<br><small><span class=""t4"">kàn</span><span class=""t3"">qǐ</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t1"">gāo</span><span class=""t4"">xìng</span>。</small><br><i>Il a l'air de ne pas être très content.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">是…的 (结构重点)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	<b>Carte en double.</b><br><br>Le même point est traité par la fiche « 是…的 — structure de mise en relief    HSK 3 ». Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">在乎 / 在意 — se soucier de / tenir compte de</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>在乎</b> = tenir à / se soucier de (émotionnel). <b>在意</b> = prendre en compte / faire attention à (plus large). Négation fréquente : 不在乎 / 不在意.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他根本不在乎别人的眼光。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">gēn</span><span class=""t3"">běn</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t1"">hū</span> <span class=""t2"">bié</span><span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">yǎn</span><span class=""t1"">guāng</span>。</small><br><i>Il se moque complètement du regard des autres.</i><br><br>• 她很在意自己的健康状况。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t4"">zì</span><span class=""t3"">jǐ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t1"">kāng</span><span class=""t4"">zhuàng</span><span class=""t4"">kuàng</span>。</small><br><i>Elle attache beaucoup d'importance à son état de santé.</i><br><br>• 这点小事你用不着太在意。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎo</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">yòng</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">zháo</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span>。</small><br><i>Pour une si petite chose, pas besoin d'y attacher trop d'importance.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">趁 — profiter du moment où / pendant que</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>趁</b> + condition favorable + action = profiter de l'occasion. Toujours suivi d'une action bénéficiaire.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 趁热打铁。<br><small><span class=""t4"">chèn</span><span class=""t4"">rè</span><span class=""t3"">dǎ</span><span class=""t3"">tiě</span>。</small><br><i>Battre le fer pendant qu'il est chaud.</i><br><br>• 趁年轻多学点东西。<br><small><span class=""t4"">chèn</span> <span class=""t2"">nián</span><span class=""t1"">qīng</span> <span class=""t1"">duō</span><span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t1"">dōng</span><span class=""t0"">xi</span>。</small><br><i>Profite de ta jeunesse pour apprendre autant que possible.</i><br><br>• 趁他不注意，她悄悄离开了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">chèn</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">zhù</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">qiāo</span><span class=""t1"">qiāo</span> <span class=""t2"">lí</span><span class=""t1"">kāi</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Pendant qu'il ne regardait pas, elle est partie discrètement.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">反正 — de toute façon / peu importe comment</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>反正</b> = de toute façon / dans tous les cas. Exprime une résignation ou une certitude peu importe les conditions.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 反正你不来，我一个人去也行。<br><small><span class=""t3"">fǎn</span><span class=""t4"">zhèng</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">xíng</span>。</small><br><i>De toute façon tu ne viens pas, j'irai seul.</i><br><br>• 反正结果都一样，你高兴就好。<br><small><span class=""t3"">fǎn</span><span class=""t4"">zhèng</span> <span class=""t2"">jié</span><span class=""t3"">guǒ</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">yàng</span>， <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">gāo</span><span class=""t4"">xìng</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>。</small><br><i>Le résultat sera le même de toute façon — tant que tu es content.</i><br><br>• 反正都要做，不如现在就开始。<br><small><span class=""t3"">fǎn</span><span class=""t4"">zhèng</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span>， <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t4"">xiàn</span><span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t1"">kāi</span><span class=""t3"">shǐ</span>。</small><br><i>De toute façon il faut le faire — autant commencer maintenant.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">没什么 — ce n'est rien / pas vraiment</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>没什么</b> + adj/V = ce n'est pas vraiment / pas grand-chose. Minimise l'importance.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这点小事没什么大不了的。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎo</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">liǎo</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span>。</small><br><i>Cette petite chose, ce n'est pas un grand problème.</i><br><br>• 失败了没什么，重新来过就好了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span>， <span class=""t2"">chóng</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t4"">guò</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Échouer, ce n'est rien — recommencer et c'est bon.</i><br><br>• 他没什么特别的地方，就是努力。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">tè</span><span class=""t2"">bié</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">dì</span><span class=""t0"">fang</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>。</small><br><i>Il n'a rien de spécial — il travaille juste dur.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">哪里 (哪儿) — pas du tout (ironie/modestie)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>哪里哪里</b> ou <b>哪里</b> seul = pas du tout (réponse modeste à un compliment). Aussi utilisé pour contredire.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• ——你汉语说得真好！——哪里哪里，还差得远。<br><small>—— <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">hàn</span><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">zhēn</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>！—— <span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span>， <span class=""t2"">hái</span> <span class=""t4"">chà</span><span class=""t0"">de</span><span class=""t3"">yuǎn</span>。</small><br><i>— Ton chinois est vraiment bien ! — Pas du tout, j'ai encore beaucoup de chemin.</i><br><br>• 他哪里是在帮忙，分明是在添乱。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t1"">bāng</span><span class=""t2"">máng</span>， <span class=""t1"">fēn</span><span class=""t2"">míng</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t1"">tiān</span><span class=""t4"">luàn</span>。</small><br><i>Il n'aide pas du tout — il ne fait que compliquer les choses.</i><br><br>• 你哪里知道我有多难过。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t0"">dao</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t2"">nán</span><span class=""t4"">guò</span>。</small><br><i>Comment pourrais-tu savoir à quel point je suis triste ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">顺便 — en passant / en même temps (occasion)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>顺便</b> = en passant / tant qu'on y est. Indique une action accessoire faite en même temps qu'une autre.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 你去超市顺便帮我买点牛奶。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t1"">chāo</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">shùn</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span> <span class=""t1"">bāng</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">mǎi</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t2"">niú</span><span class=""t3"">nǎi</span>。</small><br><i>En allant au supermarché, achète-moi du lait en passant.</i><br><br>• 我顺便问了一下价格。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t4"">shùn</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">xià</span> <span class=""t4"">jià</span><span class=""t2"">gé</span>。</small><br><i>En passant, j'ai demandé le prix.</i><br><br>• 顺便说一下，明天会议改到下午了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">shùn</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">xià</span>， <span class=""t2"">míng</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t3"">gǎi</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">xià</span><span class=""t3"">wǔ</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>En passant, la réunion de demain est déplacée à l'après-midi.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">于是 — alors / c'est ainsi que (conséquence narrative)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>于是</b> = alors / c'est ainsi que. Connecteur narratif de conséquence, souvent oral/écrit. ≠ 所以 (moins formel, causalité générale).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他等了很久没人来，于是离开了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">děng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t3"">jiǔ</span><span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span>， <span class=""t2"">yú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">lí</span><span class=""t1"">kāi</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Il a attendu longtemps sans que personne ne vienne, alors il est parti.</i><br><br>• 两人一见如故，于是成了好朋友。<br><small><span class=""t3"">liǎng</span><span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t4"">gù</span>， <span class=""t2"">yú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span> <span class=""t2"">péng</span><span class=""t0"">you</span>。</small><br><i>Dès leur première rencontre, tous deux se sont sentis comme de vieux amis, et c'est ainsi qu'ils sont devenus bons amis.</i><br><br>• 这家公司亏损严重，于是决定重组。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t1"">sī</span> <span class=""t1"">kuī</span><span class=""t3"">sǔn</span> <span class=""t2"">yán</span><span class=""t4"">zhòng</span>， <span class=""t2"">yú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">jué</span><span class=""t4"">dìng</span> <span class=""t2"">chóng</span><span class=""t3"">zǔ</span>。</small><br><i>Cette entreprise subissait de lourdes pertes, alors elle a décidé de se restructurer.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">V + 到 / 见 / 完 / 好 — compléments de résultat</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Le complément indique le <b>résultat</b> de l'action : 找 chercher → 找到 trouver ; 听 écouter → 听到 / 听见 entendre ; 看 regarder → 看到 / 看见 voir ; 完 = fini ; 好 = bien terminé. Négation avec 没.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我找到我的手机了。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">zhǎo</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">shǒu</span><span class=""t1"">jī</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>J'ai retrouvé mon téléphone.</i><br><br>• 我什么都没看到。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t4"">kàn</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span>。</small><br><i>Je n'ai rien vu du tout.</i><br><br>• 作业你做完了吗？<br><small><span class=""t4"">zuò</span><span class=""t4"">yè</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t2"">wán</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t0"">ma</span>？</small><br><i>As-tu fini tes devoirs ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">Lieu + V着 + nom — phrase d'existence</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Lieu + V + 着 + (nombre + classificateur) + nom : décrit ce qui se trouve quelque part et dans quelle position (description). Le nom est indéfini.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 床上放着一本书。<br><small><span class=""t2"">chuáng</span><span class=""t4"">shàng</span> <span class=""t4"">fàng</span><span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t3"">běn</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span>。</small><br><i>Un livre est posé sur le lit.</i><br><br>• 墙上挂着一张地图。<br><small><span class=""t2"">qiáng</span><span class=""t4"">shàng</span> <span class=""t4"">guà</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">zhāng</span> <span class=""t4"">dì</span><span class=""t2"">tú</span>。</small><br><i>Une carte est accrochée au mur.</i><br><br>• 门口站着两个人。<br><small><span class=""t2"">mén</span><span class=""t3"">kǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">zhàn</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t3"">liǎng</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span>。</small><br><i>Deux personnes se tiennent à l'entrée.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">V1着 + V2 — manière + action</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>V1 + 着 (+ objet) + V2 : V1着 indique la <b>manière</b> ou la posture dans laquelle se fait l'action principale V2.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他哭着看爱情小说。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">kū</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t4"">kàn</span> <span class=""t4"">ài</span><span class=""t2"">qíng</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎo</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>。</small><br><i>Il lit un roman d'amour en pleurant.</i><br><br>• 他在床上躺着看小说。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t2"">chuáng</span><span class=""t4"">shàng</span> <span class=""t3"">tǎng</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t4"">kàn</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎo</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>。</small><br><i>Allongé sur son lit, il lit un roman.</i><br><br>• 她笑着说：“没关系。”<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">xiào</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span>：“ <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t1"">guān</span><span class=""t0"">xi</span>。”</small><br><i>Elle dit en souriant : « Ce n'est rien. »</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">了 après le verbe / 了 en fin de phrase</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>V + 了 + objet : l'action est <b>accomplie</b>. Phrase + 了 : la <b>situation a changé</b> (nouvel état). La place de 了 change le sens. Après 没, jamais de 了.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他去中国学了中文。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t2"">guó</span> <span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t2"">wén</span>。</small><br><i>Il a étudié le chinois en Chine. (action accomplie)</i><br><br>• 他去中国学中文了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t2"">guó</span> <span class=""t2"">xué</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t2"">wén</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Il est parti étudier le chinois en Chine. (situation nouvelle)</i><br><br>• 我病了。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span><span class=""t4"">bìng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Je suis malade. (je ne l'étais pas avant)</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不但…而且… — non seulement… mais en plus…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Relation de gradation (递进关系). Même sujet : sujet + 不但…，而且…. Sujets différents : 不但 + sujet 1…，而且 + sujet 2 + 也….<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他不但会说汉语，而且会写汉字。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t4"">hàn</span><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span>， <span class=""t2"">ér</span><span class=""t3"">qiě</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t3"">xiě</span> <span class=""t4"">hàn</span><span class=""t4"">zì</span>。</small><br><i>Non seulement il parle chinois, mais en plus il sait écrire les caractères.</i><br><br>• 这个手机不但好看，而且很便宜。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t3"">shǒu</span><span class=""t1"">jī</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span><span class=""t4"">kàn</span>， <span class=""t2"">ér</span><span class=""t3"">qiě</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t2"">pián</span><span class=""t0"">yi</span>。</small><br><i>Ce téléphone est non seulement joli, mais en plus pas cher.</i><br><br>• 不但他去，而且他的朋友也去。<br><small><span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span>， <span class=""t2"">ér</span><span class=""t3"">qiě</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">péng</span><span class=""t0"">you</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span>。</small><br><i>Non seulement il y va, mais ses amis aussi.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">并不是…，而是… — ce n'est pas du tout…, mais…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>不是 A，而是 B : ce n'est pas A, c'est B. 并 devant la négation la <b>renforce</b> et contredit ce que l'on pourrait croire.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我并不是不想去，而是没有时间。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span>， <span class=""t2"">ér</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t1"">jiān</span>。</small><br><i>Ce n'est pas que je ne veuille pas y aller, c'est que je n'ai pas le temps.</i><br><br>• 他得到这份工作并不是因为他能力强，而是因为他太太是老板的女儿。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">dé</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">fèn</span> <span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">néng</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span> <span class=""t2"">qiáng</span>， <span class=""t2"">ér</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t0"">tai</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">lǎo</span><span class=""t3"">bǎn</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">nǚ</span><span class=""t2"">ér</span>。</small><br><i>S'il a obtenu ce travail, ce n'est pas du tout parce qu'il est compétent, mais parce que sa femme est la fille du patron.</i><br><br>• 这并不难。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">nán</span>。</small><br><i>Ce n'est pas difficile du tout.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">为了 — dans le but de (连接目的)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>为了 + but, + action : le but se place <b>en tête</b> de phrase (ou juste avant le verbe). Ne pas confondre avec 因为 (cause). À l'écrit on trouve aussi 为的是 après l'action.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 为了学好中文，他每天听中文广播。<br><small><span class=""t4"">wèi</span><span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t2"">wén</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">měi</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t1"">tīng</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t2"">wén</span> <span class=""t3"">guǎng</span><span class=""t1"">bō</span>。</small><br><i>Pour bien apprendre le chinois, il écoute la radio chinoise tous les jours.</i><br><br>• 为了健康，我们应该少吃盐。<br><small><span class=""t4"">wèi</span><span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t1"">kāng</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span><span class=""t0"">men</span> <span class=""t1"">yīng</span><span class=""t1"">gāi</span> <span class=""t3"">shǎo</span> <span class=""t1"">chī</span><span class=""t2"">yán</span>。</small><br><i>Pour la santé, nous devrions manger moins salé.</i><br><br>• 他这么做，为的是让父母放心。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span>， <span class=""t4"">wèi</span><span class=""t0"">de</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">ràng</span> <span class=""t4"">fù</span><span class=""t3"">mǔ</span> <span class=""t4"">fàng</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span>。</small><br><i>Il agit ainsi pour rassurer ses parents.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不过 / 其实 — mais (atténué) / en fait</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>不过 = mais, cependant, plus léger que 但是 ; toujours en tête de la seconde proposition. 其实 = en fait, en réalité (corrige une idée reçue) ; se place après le sujet ou en tête.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这家饭馆的菜很好吃，不过有点贵。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t4"">fàn</span><span class=""t3"">guǎn</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">cài</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span><span class=""t1"">chī</span>， <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">guò</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">guì</span>。</small><br><i>Les plats de ce restaurant sont délicieux, mais un peu chers.</i><br><br>• 大家都以为他很严肃，其实他很幽默。<br><small><span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t2"">wéi</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t2"">yán</span><span class=""t4"">sù</span>， <span class=""t2"">qí</span><span class=""t2"">shí</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t1"">yōu</span><span class=""t4"">mò</span>。</small><br><i>Tout le monde le croit sérieux, en fait il est très drôle.</i><br><br>• 其实我早就知道了。<br><small><span class=""t2"">qí</span><span class=""t2"">shí</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">zǎo</span><span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t0"">dao</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>En fait, je le savais depuis longtemps.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">描述一个人 — décrire une personne</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Apparence : 长得 + adj (长得很高 / 很漂亮), 个子 + 高/矮, 留着长头发, 戴眼镜. Caractère : 性格 + adj (开朗 / 内向 / 热情), 对人很 + adj (对人很友好). Goûts : 喜欢 + V, 对…感兴趣.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我的朋友个子很高，留着短头发，戴眼镜。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">péng</span><span class=""t0"">you</span> <span class=""t4"">gè</span><span class=""t0"">zi</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t1"">gāo</span>， <span class=""t2"">liú</span><span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t3"">duǎn</span> <span class=""t2"">tóu</span><span class=""t0"">fa</span>， <span class=""t4"">dài</span><span class=""t3"">yǎn</span><span class=""t4"">jìng</span>。</small><br><i>Mon ami est grand, il a les cheveux courts et porte des lunettes.</i><br><br>• 她性格开朗，对人很热情。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">xìng</span><span class=""t2"">gé</span><span class=""t1"">kāi</span><span class=""t3"">lǎng</span>， <span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t4"">rè</span><span class=""t2"">qíng</span>。</small><br><i>Elle a un caractère ouvert et elle est chaleureuse avec les gens.</i><br><br>• 他对音乐很感兴趣，周末常去听音乐会。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">yuè</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t3"">gǎn</span><span class=""t4"">xìng</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span>， <span class=""t1"">zhōu</span><span class=""t4"">mò</span> <span class=""t2"">cháng</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t1"">tīng</span> <span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">yuè</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span>。</small><br><i>Il s'intéresse beaucoup à la musique, le week-end il va souvent au concert.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">描述一个地方 — décrire un lieu</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Situation : X 位于 / 在 + lieu, 离…很近/很远. Contenu : lieu + 有 + …, 又…又…, 到处都是…. Appréciation : 风景很美, 很热闹 / 很安静, 值得一去.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我的家乡位于中国南方，离海很近。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">jiā</span><span class=""t1"">xiāng</span> <span class=""t4"">wèi</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t2"">guó</span> <span class=""t2"">nán</span><span class=""t1"">fāng</span>， <span class=""t2"">lí</span><span class=""t3"">hǎi</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t4"">jìn</span>。</small><br><i>Ma ville natale se trouve dans le sud de la Chine, tout près de la mer.</i><br><br>• 公园里有一个湖，湖边有很多树，又安静又漂亮。<br><small><span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t2"">yuán</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">hú</span>， <span class=""t2"">hú</span><span class=""t1"">biān</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t4"">shù</span>， <span class=""t4"">yòu</span> <span class=""t1"">ān</span><span class=""t4"">jìng</span> <span class=""t4"">yòu</span> <span class=""t4"">piào</span><span class=""t0"">liang</span>。</small><br><i>Dans le parc il y a un lac, au bord du lac beaucoup d'arbres ; c'est à la fois calme et joli.</i><br><br>• 这个地方风景很美，值得一去。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">dì</span><span class=""t0"">fang</span> <span class=""t1"">fēng</span><span class=""t3"">jǐng</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t3"">měi</span>， <span class=""t2"">zhí</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span>。</small><br><i>Le paysage de cet endroit est très beau, il vaut le détour.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">请假条 — le mot d'excuse (demande de congé)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Modèle : 1) destinataire + 您好！ 2) motif : 因为…，3) demande : 我想请假 + durée (请一天假 / 请假两天), 4) 望批准 (merci d'accepter), 5) formule finale 此致 / 敬礼, 6) nom (学生 X) et date. Registre poli : 您, 请, 麻烦.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 王老师，您好！因为我生病了，明天不能来上课，想请假一天，望批准。<br><small><span class=""t2"">wáng</span><span class=""t3"">lǎo</span><span class=""t1"">shī</span>， <span class=""t2"">nín</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>！ <span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t1"">shēng</span><span class=""t4"">bìng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t2"">míng</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t4"">shàng</span><span class=""t4"">kè</span>， <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t3"">qǐng</span><span class=""t4"">jià</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span>， <span class=""t4"">wàng</span> <span class=""t1"">pī</span><span class=""t3"">zhǔn</span>。</small><br><i>Bonjour Madame Wang. Comme je suis malade, je ne pourrai pas venir en cours demain ; je souhaite demander un jour de congé, merci de bien vouloir l'accepter.</i><br><br>• 我想请两天假回家看父母。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t3"">qǐng</span> <span class=""t3"">liǎng</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t4"">jià</span> <span class=""t2"">huí</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t4"">kàn</span> <span class=""t4"">fù</span><span class=""t3"">mǔ</span>。</small><br><i>Je voudrais prendre deux jours de congé pour rentrer voir mes parents.</i><br><br>• 此致敬礼。学生李明，9月20日。<br><small><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span><span class=""t4"">zhì</span><span class=""t4"">jìng</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span>。 <span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t0"">sheng</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐ</span><span class=""t2"">míng</span>，9 <span class=""t4"">yuè</span>20 <span class=""t4"">rì</span>。</small><br><i>Respectueusement. L'étudiant Li Ming, le 20 septembre.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">能不能再说一遍？— demander de répéter ou d'expliquer</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>能不能再说一遍？/ 你能再说一次吗？ : demander une répétition. 我没听懂 / 我没听清楚 : dire qu'on n'a pas compris (清楚 = clairement, un problème d'écoute ; 懂 = un problème de compréhension). 这是什么意思？: demander le sens d'un mot.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 对不起，能不能再说一遍？<br><small><span class=""t4"">duì</span><span class=""t0"">bu</span><span class=""t3"">qǐ</span>， <span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t0"">bu</span><span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span>？</small><br><i>Pardon, pouvez-vous répéter une fois de plus ?</i><br><br>• 我没听清楚，你是说几点见面？<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t1"">tīng</span> <span class=""t1"">qīng</span><span class=""t0"">chu</span>， <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t3"">jǐ</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t4"">miàn</span>？</small><br><i>Je n'ai pas bien entendu, tu dis qu'on se retrouve à quelle heure ?</i><br><br>• “举例”是什么意思？<br><small>“ <span class=""t3"">jǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>” <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t0"">si</span>？</small><br><i>Que veut dire « 举例 » ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_oral	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">换句话说 / 简单来说 — reformuler à l'oral</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>换句话说 = autrement dit (reformule ce qui vient d'être dit). 简单来说 = pour faire simple, en résumé (introduit une synthèse). 也就是说 = c'est-à-dire.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他没有直接拒绝，换句话说，他还在考虑。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t2"">zhí</span><span class=""t1"">jiē</span> <span class=""t4"">jù</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span>， <span class=""t4"">huàn</span><span class=""t4"">jù</span><span class=""t4"">huà</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t3"">kǎo</span><span class=""t4"">lǜ</span>。</small><br><i>Il n'a pas "<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">举个例子来说 — donner un exemple à l'oral</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>举个例子(来说) : formule pour introduire un exemple à l'oral. 比如 / 比如说 : par exemple (plus court, très courant). Placés en tête de la phrase d'exemple.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 举个例子来说，很多年轻人现在喜欢网购。<br><small><span class=""t3"">jǔ</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">lì</span><span class=""t0"">zi</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t2"">nián</span><span class=""t1"">qīng</span><span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t4"">xiàn</span><span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t3"">xǐ</span><span class=""t0"">huan</span> <span class=""t3"">wǎng</span><span class=""t4"">gòu</span>。</small><br><i>Pour donner un exemple, beaucoup de jeunes aiment aujourd'hui faire leurs achats en ligne.</i><br><br>• 比如说，我们可以先讨论第一个问题。<br><small><span class=""t3"">bǐ</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span><span class=""t0"">men</span> <span class=""t3"">kě</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">xiān</span> <span class=""t3"">tǎo</span><span class=""t4"">lùn</span> <span class=""t4"">dì</span><span class=""t1"">yī</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>。</small><br><i>Par exemple, on pourrait d'abord discuter de la première question.</i><br><br>• 这种情况很常见，比如在大城市。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t3"">zhǒng</span> <span class=""t2"">qíng</span><span class=""t4"">kuàng</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t2"">cháng</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span>， <span class=""t3"">bǐ</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span>。</small><br><i>Ce cas de figure est très courant, par exemple dans les grandes villes.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_oral	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">我想补充一点 — prendre la parole dans une discussion</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>我想补充一点 : ajouter une remarque à ce qui vient d'être dit. 我还有一个问题 : poser une nouvelle question. 打断一下 (可以吗) : interrompre poliment. 我先说完 : demander à finir avant de laisser l'autre parler.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我想补充一点，这篇文章还提到了…<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t3"">bǔ</span><span class=""t1"">chōng</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span>， <span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t1"">piān</span><span class=""t2"">wén</span><span class=""t1"">zhāng</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span> <span class=""t2"">tí</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>…</small><br><i>Je voudrais ajouter un point : cet article mentionne aussi…</i><br><br>• 打断一下，可以吗？我还有一个问题。<br><small><span class=""t3"">dǎ</span><span class=""t4"">duàn</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">xià</span>， <span class=""t3"">kě</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t0"">ma</span>？ <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>。</small><br><i>Je peux t'interrompre ? J'ai encore une question.</i><br><br>• 等一下，让我先说完。<br><small><span class=""t3"">děng</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">xià</span>， <span class=""t4"">ràng</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t1"">xiān</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span><span class=""t2"">wán</span>。</small><br><i>Attends, laisse-moi d'abord finir.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::3 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_oral	Chinois::Grammaire::3 · HSK 3
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">是…的 (确认información pasada)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 3-4</small>"	<b>Carte en double.</b><br><br>Le même point est traité par la fiche « 是…的 — structure de mise en relief    HSK 3 ». "<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">只有…才… — seulement si…alors…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Condition nécessaire. 只有 introduit la seule condition possible, 才 introduit le résultat. ≠ 只要…就 (condition suffisante).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 只有努力，才能成功。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>，<span class=""t2"">cái</span> <span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span>。</small><br><i>Ce n'est qu'en travaillant dur qu'on réussit.</i><br><br>• 只有你来，我才放心。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span>，<span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">cái</span> <span class=""t4"">fàng</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span>。</small><br><i>C'est seulement si tu viens que je serai rassuré(e).</i><br><br>• 只有亲身经历，才能真正理解。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">qīn</span><span class=""t1"">shēn</span><span class=""t1"">jīng</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>， <span class=""t2"">cái</span><span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t1"">zhēn</span><span class=""t4"">zhèng</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐ</span><span class=""t3"">jiě</span>。</small><br><i>Ce n'est qu'en le vivant soi-même qu'on peut vraiment comprendre.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">只要…就… — du moment que…alors…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Condition suffisante : 只要 + condition, 就 + résultat. ≠ 只有…才 (condition nécessaire).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 只要你喜欢，就可以买。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">xǐ</span><span class=""t0"">huan</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t3"">kě</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">mǎi</span>。</small><br><i>Du moment que tu l'aimes, tu peux l'acheter.</i><br><br>• 只要坚持，就能成功。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t1"">jiān</span><span class=""t2"">chí</span>，<span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span>。</small><br><i>Du moment qu'on persévère, on réussira.</i><br><br>• 只要有网，就能工作。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">wǎng</span>，<span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span>。</small><br><i>Du moment qu'il y a internet, je peux travailler.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">既然…就… — puisque…alors…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>既然 introduit un fait déjà admis ou accompli, 就 en tire la conséquence logique.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 既然来了，就好好玩吧。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jì</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>，<span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span> <span class=""t2"">wán</span> <span class=""t0"">ba</span>。</small><br><i>Puisque tu es là, profites-en bien.</i><br><br>• 既然你不喜欢，就不要勉强了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jì</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t3"">xǐ</span><span class=""t0"">huan</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t3"">miǎn</span><span class=""t3"">qiǎng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Puisque tu n'aimes pas ça, ne te force pas.</i><br><br>• 既然决定了，就不要后悔。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jì</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t2"">jué</span><span class=""t4"">dìng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t4"">hòu</span><span class=""t3"">huǐ</span>。</small><br><i>Puisque c'est décidé, ne regrette pas.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">无论/不管…都/也… — quoi que / quel que soit</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Concession universelle : quelles que soient les conditions. 无论/不管 + mot interrogatif (ou alternative A/B) + 都/也.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 无论多忙，都要吃饭。<br><small><span class=""t2"">wú</span><span class=""t4"">lùn</span> <span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t2"">máng</span>，<span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t1"">chī</span><span class=""t4"">fàn</span>。</small><br><i>Si occupé(e) que l'on soit, il faut manger.</i><br><br>• 不管怎么说，她都是对的。<br><small><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">guǎn</span><span class=""t3"">zěn</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span>。</small><br><i>Quoi qu'on dise, elle a raison.</i><br><br>• 无论成功失败，都是经验。<br><small><span class=""t2"">wú</span><span class=""t4"">lùn</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span>， <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">jīng</span><span class=""t4"">yàn</span>。</small><br><i>Qu'on réussisse ou échoue, c'est de l'expérience.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">与其…不如… — plutôt que de…mieux vaut…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Exprime une préférence. 与其 introduit l'option moins souhaitable, 不如 introduit l'option préférable.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 与其等待，不如行动。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span> <span class=""t3"">děng</span><span class=""t4"">dài</span>，<span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t2"">xíng</span><span class=""t4"">dòng</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt qu'attendre, mieux vaut agir.</i><br><br>• 与其买新的，不如修一修。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span> <span class=""t3"">mǎi</span> <span class=""t1"">xīn</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span>， <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t1"">xiū</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">xiū</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt qu'en acheter un nouveau, mieux vaut le réparer.</i><br><br>• 与其抱怨，不如改变。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span> <span class=""t4"">bào</span><span class=""t4"">yuàn</span>，<span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t3"">gǎi</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt que de se plaindre, mieux vaut changer les choses.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">即使/哪怕…也… — même si…quand même…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Concession hypothétique forte. 即使/哪怕 introduit une condition extrême hypothétique, 也 exprime le résultat qui tient quand même.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 即使下雨，他也会来。<br><small><span class=""t2"">jí</span><span class=""t3"">shǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">xià</span><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span>，<span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span>。</small><br><i>Même s'il pleut, il viendra quand même.</i><br><br>• 哪怕再难，也要坚持。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t4"">pà</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t2"">nán</span>，<span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t1"">jiān</span><span class=""t2"">chí</span>。</small><br><i>Aussi difficile que ce soit, il faut persévérer.</i><br><br>• 即使失败，也不后悔。<br><small><span class=""t2"">jí</span><span class=""t3"">shǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span>，<span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">hòu</span><span class=""t3"">huǐ</span>。</small><br><i>Même en cas d'échec, je ne regrette pas.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">对…来说 vs 对…而言 — pour quelqu'un / selon</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>对…来说</b> = pour (point de vue de quelqu'un, registre courant). <b>对…而言</b> = pour / du point de vue de (registre plus soutenu/écrit).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 对孩子来说，玩比学习更重要。<br><small><span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span><span class=""t0"">zi</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t2"">wán</span><span class=""t3"">bǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t2"">xí</span> <span class=""t4"">gèng</span> <span class=""t4"">zhòng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span>。</small><br><i>Pour les enfants, jouer est plus important qu'étudier.</i><br><br>• 对初学者而言，这本书太难了。<br><small><span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t1"">chū</span><span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t3"">zhě</span> <span class=""t2"">ér</span><span class=""t2"">yán</span>， <span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t3"">běn</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t2"">nán</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Pour les débutants, ce livre est trop difficile.</i><br><br>• 对我来说，健康比金钱更重要。<br><small><span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t1"">kāng</span> <span class=""t3"">bǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">jīn</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span> <span class=""t4"">gèng</span> <span class=""t4"">zhòng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span>。</small><br><i>Pour moi, la santé est plus importante que l'argent.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">随着…（的发展）— avec / au fur et à mesure que</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>随着</b> + nom/proposition = au fur et à mesure que / avec l'évolution de. Exprime une évolution parallèle.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 随着年龄增长，他越来越成熟。<br><small><span class=""t2"">suí</span><span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t2"">nián</span><span class=""t2"">líng</span> <span class=""t1"">zēng</span><span class=""t3"">zhǎng</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">yuè</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span><span class=""t4"">yuè</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t2"">shú</span>。</small><br><i>Avec l'avancée en âge, il devient de plus en plus mature.</i><br><br>• 随着技术的进步，生活变得更方便。<br><small><span class=""t2"">suí</span><span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t4"">jì</span><span class=""t4"">shù</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">jìn</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span>， <span class=""t1"">shēng</span><span class=""t2"">huó</span> <span class=""t4"">biàn</span><span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">gèng</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span>。</small><br><i>Avec les progrès techniques, la vie devient plus commode.</i><br><br>• 随着问题的解决，大家放松了。<br><small><span class=""t2"">suí</span><span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span>， <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t4"">fàng</span><span class=""t1"">sōng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Au fur et à mesure que le problème s'est résolu, tout le monde s'est détendu.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不管…都 vs 无论…都 — quoi que / quel que soit</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	<b>Carte en double.</b><br><br>Le même point est traité par la fiche « 无论/不管…都/也… — quoi que / quel que soit    HSK 4 ». Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">V着V着 — tout en V-ant / alors qu'on était en train de V (un changement survient)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Répétition du verbe + 着 pour exprimer une action continue qui mène à un changement ou une transition.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他走着走着，突然停了下来。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">zǒu</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t3"">zǒu</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span>， <span class=""t1"">tū</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t2"">tíng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">xià</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span>。</small><br><i>Alors qu'il marchait, il s'est soudain arrêté.</i><br><br>• 她哭着哭着，笑了起来。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">kū</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t1"">kū</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span>， <span class=""t4"">xiào</span><span class=""t0"">le</span><span class=""t3"">qǐ</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span>。</small><br><i>Alors qu'elle pleurait, elle a fini par se mettre à rire.</i><br><br>• 说着说着就聊到了深夜。<br><small><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t2"">liáo</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t1"">shēn</span><span class=""t4"">yè</span>。</small><br><i>À force de parler, la conversation s'est prolongée jusqu'à tard dans la nuit.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 corrige_2026	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">V着V着 — à force de V / en continuant à V</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	<b>Carte remplacée.</b><br><br>Une faute dans l'énoncé a été corrigée : la version juste est une nouvelle carte du même paquet. Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">都…了 — déjà (insistance sur l'état actuel)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>都…了 souligne que la situation a atteint un stade avancé ou surprenant. 都 intensifie, 了 marque le nouvel état.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 都十二点了，还不睡？<br><small><span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t4"">èr</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t2"">hái</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">shuì</span>？</small><br><i>Il est déjà minuit, tu ne dors toujours pas ?</i><br><br>• 她都哭了，你还不道歉？<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t1"">kū</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t4"">qiàn</span>？</small><br><i>Elle pleure déjà, tu ne t'excuses toujours pas ?</i><br><br>• 饭都凉了，快来吃吧。<br><small><span class=""t4"">fàn</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">liáng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t4"">kuài</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t1"">chī</span> <span class=""t0"">ba</span>。</small><br><i>La nourriture est déjà froide, viens vite manger.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">哪怕 + 最小条件 + 也… — même si (minimal)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>哪怕</b> introduit la condition minimale concédée, <b>也</b> indique que le résultat tient quand même. Plus dramatique/émotionnel que 即使.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 哪怕一分钟，我也不想等。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t4"">pà</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">fēn</span><span class=""t1"">zhōng</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t3"">děng</span>。</small><br><i>Je ne veux pas attendre, même une minute.</i><br><br>• 哪怕是最简单的事，她也要做到最好。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t4"">pà</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">zuì</span> <span class=""t3"">jiǎn</span><span class=""t1"">dān</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">zuì</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>。</small><br><i>Même pour la chose la plus simple, elle veut faire de son mieux.</i><br><br>• 哪怕再难，只要努力就有希望。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t4"">pà</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t2"">nán</span>， <span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">xī</span><span class=""t4"">wàng</span>。</small><br><i>Aussi difficile que ce soit, si on travaille, il y a de l'espoir.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不是…就是… / 要么…要么… — soit…soit…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>不是A就是B</b> = soit A soit B (ensemble exhaustif).<br><b>要么A要么B</b> = soit A soit B (choix entre deux options, souvent exclusif).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他不是在打游戏，就是在刷手机。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t3"">dǎ</span><span class=""t2"">yóu</span><span class=""t4"">xì</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t1"">shuā</span> <span class=""t3"">shǒu</span><span class=""t1"">jī</span>。</small><br><i>Il joue soit aux jeux vidéo, soit scrolle sur son téléphone.</i><br><br>• 要么接受，要么拒绝，没有第三条路。<br><small><span class=""t4"">yào</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t1"">jiē</span><span class=""t4"">shòu</span>， <span class=""t4"">yào</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">jù</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span>， <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">dì</span><span class=""t1"">sān</span><span class=""t2"">tiáo</span> <span class=""t4"">lù</span>。</small><br><i>Soit on accepte, soit on refuse — pas de troisième option.</i><br><br>• 这件事不是你的错就是我的错。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">cuò</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">cuò</span>。</small><br><i>Soit c'est ta faute, soit c'est la mienne.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">对…有益/有害/有影响 — avoir un effet sur</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>对 + domaine + 有益/有害/有影响/有帮助 = être bénéfique/nuisible/avoir une influence/être utile à.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 适量运动对身体有益。<br><small><span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t4"">liàng</span> <span class=""t4"">yùn</span><span class=""t4"">dòng</span> <span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t1"">shēn</span><span class=""t3"">tǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span>。</small><br><i>L'exercice modéré est bénéfique pour le corps.</i><br><br>• 过度依赖手机对睡眠有害。<br><small><span class=""t4"">guò</span><span class=""t4"">dù</span> <span class=""t1"">yī</span><span class=""t4"">lài</span> <span class=""t3"">shǒu</span><span class=""t1"">jī</span> <span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t4"">shuì</span><span class=""t2"">mián</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t4"">hài</span>。</small><br><i>Une dépendance excessive au téléphone nuit au sommeil.</i><br><br>• 这个决定对整个团队都有影响。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">jué</span><span class=""t4"">dìng</span> <span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t3"">zhěng</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">tuán</span><span class=""t4"">duì</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐng</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span>。</small><br><i>Cette décision a une incidence sur toute l'équipe.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">一方面…另一方面… — d'un côté…de l'autre…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Présente deux aspects d'une même situation. Souvent utilisé pour exprimer une tension ou complémentarité.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 一方面要赚钱，另一方面也要照顾家庭。<br><small><span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">miàn</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t4"">zhuàn</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span>， <span class=""t4"">lìng</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">miàn</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t4"">zhào</span><span class=""t4"">gù</span> <span class=""t1"">jiā</span><span class=""t2"">tíng</span>。</small><br><i>D'un côté, il faut gagner de l'argent ;
