de l'autre, il faut aussi s'occuper de la famille.</i><br><br>• 这个政策一方面降低了成本，另一方面也带来了新风险。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">zhèng</span><span class=""t4"">cè</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">miàn</span> <span class=""t4"">jiàng</span><span class=""t1"">dī</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t3"">běn</span>， <span class=""t4"">lìng</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">miàn</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">dài</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t1"">xīn</span> <span class=""t1"">fēng</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎn</span>。</small><br><i>Cette politique réduit d'un côté les coûts, mais introduit de l'autre de nouveaux risques.</i><br><br>• 她一方面很感动，另一方面又有些不知所措。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">miàn</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t3"">gǎn</span><span class=""t4"">dòng</span>， <span class=""t4"">lìng</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">miàn</span> <span class=""t4"">yòu</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t1"">xiē</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t4"">cuò</span>。</small><br><i>D'un côté, elle était très touchée ; de l'autre, elle ne savait pas trop quoi faire.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不得不 — être obligé de / ne pas pouvoir ne pas</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>不得不</b> + V = être contraint de faire (double négation → obligation inévitable). Résignation ou nécessité forcée.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他不得不接受这个结果。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t1"">jiē</span><span class=""t4"">shòu</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">jié</span><span class=""t3"">guǒ</span>。</small><br><i>Il était obligé d'accepter ce résultat.</i><br><br>• 面对现实，我们不得不做出改变。<br><small><span class=""t4"">miàn</span><span class=""t4"">duì</span><span class=""t4"">xiàn</span><span class=""t2"">shí</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span><span class=""t0"">men</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span><span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span><span class=""t1"">chū</span> <span class=""t3"">gǎi</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span>。</small><br><i>Face à la réalité, nous n'avons pas d'autre choix que de changer.</i><br><br>• 她不得不承认自己错了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t4"">rèn</span> <span class=""t4"">zì</span><span class=""t3"">jǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">cuò</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Elle était forcée d'admettre qu'elle avait tort.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">非…不可 — il faut absolument / impossible de ne pas</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>非 + V/adj + 不可 = obligation absolue (ne peut pas ne pas faire). Exprime souvent la nécessité inévitable ou le désir impérieux.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这道题非你解答不可。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span> <span class=""t1"">fēi</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t2"">dá</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">kě</span>。</small><br><i>C'est toi et personne d'autre qui peut résoudre ce problème.</i><br><br>• 他非赢不可，否则后果严重。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">fēi</span><span class=""t2"">yíng</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">kě</span>， <span class=""t3"">fǒu</span><span class=""t2"">zé</span> <span class=""t4"">hòu</span><span class=""t3"">guǒ</span><span class=""t2"">yán</span><span class=""t4"">zhòng</span>。</small><br><i>Il doit absolument gagner, sinon les conséquences seront graves.</i><br><br>• 这本书非读不可。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t3"">běn</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span> <span class=""t1"">fēi</span><span class=""t2"">dú</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">kě</span>。</small><br><i>Ce livre, il faut absolument le lire.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">难道 — est-ce vraiment possible que (rhétorique)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>难道</b> introduit une question rhétorique exprimant la surprise, l'incrédulité ou le blâme. La réponse implicite est toujours négative.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 难道你不知道今天有考试吗？<br><small><span class=""t2"">nán</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t0"">dao</span> <span class=""t1"">jīn</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">kǎo</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t0"">ma</span>？</small><br><i>Tu ne savais vraiment pas qu'il y avait un examen aujourd'hui ?</i><br><br>• 难道我说的话你一句都不信吗？<br><small><span class=""t2"">nán</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span><span class=""t4"">huà</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">jù</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">xìn</span> <span class=""t0"">ma</span>？</small><br><i>Est-ce que tu ne crois vraiment pas un seul mot de ce que je dis ?</i><br><br>• 这件事难道真的没有解决办法吗？<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">nán</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t1"">zhēn</span><span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span><span class=""t4"">bàn</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span> <span class=""t0"">ma</span>？</small><br><i>Y a-t-il vraiment aucune façon de résoudre ça ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">到底 vs 究竟 (dans les questions) — au fond / en définitive</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>到底</b> et <b>究竟</b> s'utilisent tous les deux pour exiger une réponse définitive dans les questions. <b>到底</b> = oral/émotionnel. <b>究竟</b> = plus neutre/formel.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 你到底想要什么？<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t3"">dǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span>？</small><br><i>Qu'est-ce que tu veux au fond ?</i><br><br>• 这究竟是怎么回事？<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t1"">jiū</span><span class=""t4"">jìng</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">zěn</span><span class=""t0"">me</span><span class=""t2"">huí</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span>？</small><br><i>Qu'est-ce qui se passe réellement ?</i><br><br>• 他到底去没去，没人说得清楚。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t3"">dǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span>， <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">qīng</span><span class=""t0"">chu</span>。</small><br><i>S'il y est allé ou non, personne ne peut le dire clairement.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 corrige_2026	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">到底 (assertif) vs 究竟 — au fond / en définitive</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	<b>Carte remplacée.</b><br><br>Une faute dans l'énoncé a été corrigée : la version juste est une nouvelle carte du même paquet. Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">竟然 / 居然 — étonnamment / contre toute attente</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>竟然</b> / <b>居然</b> = étonnamment (résultat inattendu, souvent négatif ou surprenant). Toujours devant le verbe. Peuvent être interchangeables dans la plupart des contextes.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他竟然在考场里睡着了！<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">jìng</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t3"">kǎo</span><span class=""t3"">chǎng</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">shuì</span><span class=""t2"">zháo</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>！</small><br><i>Incroyable, il s'est endormi dans la salle d'examen !</i><br><br>• 这本书居然卖了三百万册！<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t3"">běn</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span> <span class=""t1"">jū</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t4"">mài</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t1"">sān</span> <span class=""t3"">bǎi</span><span class=""t4"">wàn</span><span class=""t4"">cè</span>！</small><br><i>Ce livre s'est vendu à trois millions d'exemplaires, incroyable !</i><br><br>• 她居然一个人把那个项目做完了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">jū</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t3"">bǎ</span> <span class=""t4"">nà</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">xiàng</span><span class=""t4"">mù</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t2"">wán</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Elle a étonnamment terminé ce projet toute seule.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">何必 — pourquoi s'en faire / inutile de (rhétorique)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>何必</b> + V = pourquoi s'en faire à propos de / inutile de. Exprime l'inutilité d'une action.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 何必这么认真呢，放松一点。<br><small><span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t4"">bì</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">rèn</span><span class=""t1"">zhēn</span> <span class=""t0"">ne</span>， <span class=""t4"">fàng</span><span class=""t1"">sōng</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span>。</small><br><i>Pourquoi être aussi sérieux ? Détends-toi un peu.</i><br><br>• 这件事已经过去了，何必再提？<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t1"">jīng</span> <span class=""t4"">guò</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t4"">bì</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t2"">tí</span>？</small><br><i>C'est passé — pourquoi en reparler ?</i><br><br>• 那个人不值得，何必为他难过。<br><small><span class=""t4"">nà</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t2"">zhí</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span>， <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t4"">bì</span> <span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">nán</span><span class=""t4"">guò</span>。</small><br><i>Cette personne n'en vaut pas la peine — pourquoi se rendre triste pour elle ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">就算 / 哪怕…也… — même si (concession orale)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>就算</b> = même si (hypothèse, oral). <b>哪怕</b> = même si (émotionnel, dramatique). Les deux précèdent une proposition hypothétique, suivies de 也.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 就算失败了，也没关系，我们再来一次。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">suàn</span> <span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t1"">guān</span><span class=""t0"">xi</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span><span class=""t0"">men</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">cì</span>。</small><br><i>Même si on échoue, pas grave — on recommence.</i><br><br>• 哪怕只有一点点希望，我也不会放弃。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t4"">pà</span> <span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t1"">xī</span><span class=""t4"">wàng</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t4"">fàng</span><span class=""t4"">qì</span>。</small><br><i>Même s'il n'y a qu'une toute petite espérance, je n'abandonnerai pas.</i><br><br>• 就算再难，他都会想办法解决。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">suàn</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t2"">nán</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t4"">bàn</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span>。</small><br><i>Aussi difficile que ce soit, il trouvera un moyen de le résoudre.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">一来…二来… — d'une part…d'autre part… (raisons)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Liste deux raisons ou deux aspects. Peut se prolonger avec 三来.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 我不想去，一来太贵了，二来也没时间。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span>， <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t4"">guì</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t4"">èr</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t1"">jiān</span>。</small><br><i>Je ne veux pas y aller — d'une part c'est trop cher, d'autre part je n'ai pas le temps.</i><br><br>• 这样做一来方便，二来也省钱。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">yàng</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span>， <span class=""t4"">èr</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t3"">shěng</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span>。</small><br><i>Faire ainsi est d'une part pratique, d'autre part économique.</i><br><br>• 她选这份工作，一来薪资不错，二来离家近。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">xuǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">fèn</span> <span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span>， <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t1"">xīn</span><span class=""t1"">zī</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">cuò</span>， <span class=""t4"">èr</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t2"">lí</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span><span class=""t4"">jìn</span>。</small><br><i>Elle a choisi ce travail — d'une part le salaire est bon, d'autre part c'est près de chez elle.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">大不了 — au pire / ce n'est pas grave si</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>大不了</b> + V = au pire, le pire qui puisse arriver est V. Exprime la relativisation de l'enjeu.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 大不了不去，有什么了不起的。<br><small><span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">liǎo</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span>， <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t3"">liǎo</span><span class=""t0"">bu</span><span class=""t3"">qǐ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span>。</small><br><i>Au pire on n'y va pas — qu'est-ce que ça change ?</i><br><br>• 失败了大不了再来一次。<br><small><span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">liǎo</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t2"">lái</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">cì</span>。</small><br><i>Si on échoue, au pire on recommence.</i><br><br>• 大不了被批评几句，没什么大事。<br><small><span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">liǎo</span> <span class=""t4"">bèi</span> <span class=""t1"">pī</span><span class=""t2"">píng</span> <span class=""t3"">jǐ</span><span class=""t4"">jù</span>， <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span>。</small><br><i>Au pire on se fait réprimander — c'est pas la fin du monde.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">免得 — pour éviter que / pour ne pas que</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>免得</b> + résultat à éviter = afin d'éviter que. Toujours après une action préventive.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 早点出发，免得堵车迟到。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zǎo</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t1"">chū</span><span class=""t1"">fā</span>， <span class=""t3"">miǎn</span><span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">dǔ</span><span class=""t1"">chē</span> <span class=""t2"">chí</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span>。</small><br><i>Pars tôt pour éviter les embouteillages et d'arriver en retard.</i><br><br>• 把规则说清楚，免得误会。<br><small><span class=""t3"">bǎ</span> <span class=""t1"">guī</span><span class=""t2"">zé</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t1"">qīng</span><span class=""t0"">chu</span>， <span class=""t3"">miǎn</span><span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">wù</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span>。</small><br><i>Explique bien les règles pour éviter les malentendus.</i><br><br>• 她没有多说，免得引起不必要的担心。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t3"">miǎn</span><span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐn</span><span class=""t3"">qǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">bì</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">dān</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span>。</small><br><i>Elle n'a pas dit grand-chose pour éviter d'inquiéter inutilement.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不外乎 / 无非 — rien d'autre que / c'est tout simplement</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>不外乎</b> / <b>无非</b> = rien d'autre que / c'est tout simplement. Résume ce qui suit comme étant limité à cela.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他的成功不外乎两点：努力和运气。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">wài</span><span class=""t1"">hū</span> <span class=""t3"">liǎng</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span>： <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span> <span class=""t4"">yùn</span><span class=""t4"">qì</span>。</small><br><i>Son succès ne tient à rien d'autre que deux choses : le travail et la chance.</i><br><br>• 他无非是想引起注意罢了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">wú</span><span class=""t1"">fēi</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐn</span><span class=""t3"">qǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">zhù</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t4"">bà</span><span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Il veut simplement attirer l'attention, rien de plus.</i><br><br>• 无非就是钱的问题，别想太多了。<br><small><span class=""t2"">wú</span><span class=""t1"">fēi</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">qián</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>， <span class=""t2"">bié</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t1"">duō</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Ce n'est vraiment qu'une question d'argent — ne te prends pas trop la tête.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">由于…，(因此/所以)… — cause, registre écrit (因果关系)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>由于 + cause, 因此 / 所以 + conséquence : équivalent écrit et plus formel de 因为…所以…. 因此 = par conséquent (peut ouvrir une phrase seul). Ordre : la cause d'abord.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 由于天气不好，比赛推迟了。<br><small><span class=""t2"">yóu</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t1"">tiān</span><span class=""t4"">qì</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>， <span class=""t3"">bǐ</span><span class=""t4"">sài</span> <span class=""t1"">tuī</span><span class=""t2"">chí</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>En raison du mauvais temps, la compétition a été reportée.</i><br><br>• 他工作很努力，因此进步很快。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>， <span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">jìn</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span><span class=""t4"">kuài</span>。</small><br><i>Il travaille dur, par conséquent il progresse vite.</i><br><br>• 由于时间有限，我们只能先讨论最重要的问题。<br><small><span class=""t2"">yóu</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t1"">jiān</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t4"">xiàn</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span><span class=""t0"">men</span> <span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t1"">xiān</span> <span class=""t3"">tǎo</span><span class=""t4"">lùn</span> <span class=""t4"">zuì</span> <span class=""t4"">zhòng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>。</small><br><i>Le temps étant limité, nous ne pouvons discuter d'abord que de la question la plus importante.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">既…又… — à la fois… et… (并列关系, écrit)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>既 + A + 又 + B : deux qualités ou deux actions qui coexistent, même sujet. Plus écrit que 又…又…. Variante : 既…也….<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这个办法既简单又有效。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">bàn</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span> <span class=""t4"">jì</span> <span class=""t3"">jiǎn</span><span class=""t1"">dān</span> <span class=""t4"">yòu</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t4"">xiào</span>。</small><br><i>Cette méthode est à la fois simple et efficace.</i><br><br>• 她既会说英语，又会说日语。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">jì</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t1"">yīng</span><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span>， <span class=""t4"">yòu</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t4"">rì</span><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span>。</small><br><i>Elle parle à la fois anglais et japonais.</i><br><br>• 网购既方便又便宜。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǎng</span><span class=""t4"">gòu</span> <span class=""t4"">jì</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span> <span class=""t4"">yòu</span> <span class=""t2"">pián</span><span class=""t0"">yi</span>。</small><br><i>Les achats en ligne sont à la fois pratiques et bon marché.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">首先…，其次…，最后… / 总之 — organiser un paragraphe</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Pour énumérer des arguments à l'écrit : 首先 (d'abord), 其次 (ensuite), 再次 / 另外 (de plus), 最后 (enfin). 总之 = en somme, pour conclure. Chaque connecteur ouvre une phrase, suivi d'une virgule.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 首先，网购很方便；其次，价格比较便宜；最后，选择也更多。<br><small><span class=""t3"">shǒu</span><span class=""t1"">xiān</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǎng</span><span class=""t4"">gòu</span> <span class=""t3"">hěn</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span>； <span class=""t2"">qí</span><span class=""t4"">cì</span>， <span class=""t4"">jià</span><span class=""t2"">gé</span> <span class=""t3"">bǐ</span><span class=""t4"">jiào</span> <span class=""t2"">pián</span><span class=""t0"">yi</span>； <span class=""t4"">zuì</span><span class=""t4"">hòu</span>， <span class=""t3"">xuǎn</span><span class=""t2"">zé</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">gèng</span> <span class=""t1"">duō</span>。</small><br><i>D'abord, les achats en ligne sont pratiques ; ensuite, les prix sont plutôt bas ; enfin, le choix est plus grand.</i><br><br>• 总之，学习语言需要时间和耐心。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zǒng</span><span class=""t1"">zhī</span>， <span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t2"">xí</span> <span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">yán</span> <span class=""t1"">xū</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t1"">jiān</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span> <span class=""t4"">nài</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span>。</small><br><i>En somme, apprendre une langue demande du temps et de la patience.</i><br><br>• 另外，别忘了带护照。<br><small><span class=""t4"">lìng</span><span class=""t4"">wài</span>， <span class=""t2"">bié</span><span class=""t4"">wàng</span><span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">dài</span> <span class=""t4"">hù</span><span class=""t4"">zhào</span>。</small><br><i>De plus, n'oublie pas de prendre ton passeport.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">电子邮件 — l'e-mail formel</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 4</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>主题 (objet) court. Ouverture : 尊敬的 / 亲爱的 + nom + ：您好！ Corps : 我是…，我想… / 请问…. Pièce jointe : 附件是…. Clôture : 期待您的回复 (dans l'attente de votre réponse), 祝好 / 祝工作顺利, puis nom et date.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 尊敬的张老师：您好！我是二年级的学生李华。<br><small><span class=""t1"">zūn</span><span class=""t4"">jìng</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">zhāng</span><span class=""t3"">lǎo</span><span class=""t1"">shī</span>： <span class=""t2"">nín</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>！ <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">èr</span><span class=""t2"">nián</span><span class=""t2"">jí</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">xué</span><span class=""t0"">sheng</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐ</span><span class=""t2"">huá</span>。</small><br><i>Cher Professeur Zhang, bonjour. Je suis Li Hua, étudiant de deuxième année.</i><br><br>• 附件是我的作业，请您查收。<br><small><span class=""t4"">fù</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span><span class=""t4"">yè</span>， <span class=""t3"">qǐng</span> <span class=""t2"">nín</span> <span class=""t2"">chá</span><span class=""t1"">shōu</span>。</small><br><i>Vous trouverez mon devoir en pièce jointe, je vous prie de bien vouloir le recevoir.</i><br><br>• 期待您的回复。祝好！<br><small><span class=""t1"">qī</span><span class=""t4"">dài</span> <span class=""t2"">nín</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">huí</span><span class=""t4"">fù</span>。 <span class=""t4"">zhù</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>！</small><br><i>Dans l'attente de votre réponse. Bien cordialement.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::4 grammaire deck_v2 ajout_2026 cours_L2 L2_renforcement_ecrit	Chinois::Grammaire::4 · HSK 4
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">之所以…是因为… — si…c'est parce que…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>之所以 + résultat, 是因为 + cause. Explique la raison d'un fait. Registre écrit/soutenu.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 她之所以成功，是因为不断努力。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span>， <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">duàn</span> <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>。</small><br><i>Si elle réussit, c'est parce qu'elle travaille sans cesse.</i><br><br>• 之所以难，是因为没有基础。<br><small><span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">nán</span>， <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">jī</span><span class=""t3"">chǔ</span>。</small><br><i>Si c'est difficile, c'est parce qu'il n'y a pas de bases.</i><br><br>• 公司之所以发展快，是因为管理得当。<br><small><span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t1"">sī</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">fā</span><span class=""t3"">zhǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">kuài</span>， <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t3"">guǎn</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">dé</span><span class=""t4"">dàng</span>。</small><br><i>Si l'entreprise se développe vite, c'est grâce à une bonne gestion.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">尽管…但… — bien que (fort) / malgré…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>尽管 (même si / bien que) est plus fort que 虽然. Insiste sur le fait que la concession est importante mais n'empêche pas le résultat.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 尽管困难重重，他仍坚持了下来。<br><small><span class=""t3"">jǐn</span><span class=""t3"">guǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">kùn</span><span class=""t2"">nán</span><span class=""t2"">chóng</span><span class=""t2"">chóng</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">réng</span> <span class=""t1"">jiān</span><span class=""t2"">chí</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">xià</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span>。</small><br><i>Malgré d'innombrables difficultés, il a persévéré.</i><br><br>• 尽管天气不好，活动照常进行。<br><small><span class=""t3"">jǐn</span><span class=""t3"">guǎn</span> <span class=""t1"">tiān</span><span class=""t4"">qì</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">hǎo</span>， <span class=""t2"">huó</span><span class=""t4"">dòng</span> <span class=""t4"">zhào</span><span class=""t2"">cháng</span><span class=""t4"">jìn</span><span class=""t2"">xíng</span>。</small><br><i>Malgré le mauvais temps, l'événement s'est tenu normalement.</i><br><br>• 尽管如此，她从未放弃。<br><small><span class=""t3"">jǐn</span><span class=""t3"">guǎn</span> <span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span>，<span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">cóng</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t4"">fàng</span><span class=""t4"">qì</span>。</small><br><i>Malgré tout cela, elle n'a jamais abandonné.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">固然…但/然而… — certes…mais…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>固然 reconnaît un fait comme vrai mais en nuance l'importance. Registre soutenu/écrit.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 成绩固然重要，但品德更重要。<br><small><span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t4"">jì</span> <span class=""t4"">gù</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t4"">zhòng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span>， <span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t3"">pǐn</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span> <span class=""t4"">gèng</span> <span class=""t4"">zhòng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span>。</small><br><i>Les résultats sont certes importants, mais le caractère l'est encore plus.</i><br><br>• 金钱固然有用，却不是一切。<br><small><span class=""t1"">jīn</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span> <span class=""t4"">gù</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t4"">yòng</span>， <span class=""t4"">què</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">qiè</span>。</small><br><i>L'argent est certes utile, mais ce n'est pas tout.</i><br><br>• 这个方法固然有效，但有风险。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span> <span class=""t4"">gù</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t4"">xiào</span>， <span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">fēng</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎn</span>。</small><br><i>Cette méthode est certes efficace, mais comporte des risques.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">宁可/宁愿…也不… — préférer X plutôt que de Y</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>宁可/宁愿 + option choisie + 也不 + option rejetée. Exprime un choix difficile ou un sacrifice.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 她宁可饿着，也不吃那道菜。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">nìng</span><span class=""t3"">kě</span> <span class=""t4"">è</span> <span class=""t0"">zhe</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t1"">chī</span> <span class=""t4"">nà</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t4"">cài</span>。</small><br><i>Elle préfère rester affamée plutôt que de manger ce plat.</i><br><br>• 宁愿吃苦，也不愿意说谎。<br><small><span class=""t4"">nìng</span><span class=""t4"">yuàn</span> <span class=""t1"">chī</span><span class=""t3"">kǔ</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">yuàn</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span><span class=""t3"">huǎng</span>。</small><br><i>Mieux vaut souffrir que de mentir.</i><br><br>• 他宁可一个人，也不将就。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">nìng</span><span class=""t3"">kě</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t1"">jiāng</span> <span class=""t4"">jiù</span>。</small><br><i>Il préfère rester seul plutôt que de se contenter de quelqu'un qui ne lui convient pas.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不是…就是… — soit…soit… / ou bien…ou bien…</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	<b>Carte en double.</b><br><br>Le même point est traité par la fiche « 不是…就是… / 要么…要么… — soit…soit…    HSK 4 ». Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">以…为… — prendre X comme Y / considérer X comme Y</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Structure formelle/littéraire. 以 + moyen/objet, 为 + but/qualification.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 以诚信为本。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t4"">xìn</span> <span class=""t2"">wéi</span> <span class=""t3"">běn</span>。</small><br><i>Prendre l'intégrité comme fondement.</i><br><br>• 以结果为导向。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">jié</span><span class=""t3"">guǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">wéi</span> <span class=""t3"">dǎo</span><span class=""t4"">xiàng</span>。</small><br><i>Être orienté résultats.</i><br><br>• 以客户需求为中心。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">kè</span><span class=""t4"">hù</span> <span class=""t1"">xū</span><span class=""t2"">qiú</span> <span class=""t2"">wéi</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span>。</small><br><i>Placer les besoins du client au centre.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">何况 — à plus forte raison / sans parler de</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Renforce un argument par escalade. Si A est vrai, alors B (plus extrême) est encore plus vrai.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这么简单的事我都不会，何况他呢。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t3"">jiǎn</span><span class=""t1"">dān</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span>， <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t4"">kuàng</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t0"">ne</span>。</small><br><i>Si moi je ne sais pas faire une chose aussi simple, sans parler de lui.</i><br><br>• 大人都累了，何况孩子。<br><small><span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t4"">lèi</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>，<span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t4"">kuàng</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span><span class=""t0"">zi</span>。</small><br><i>Si même les adultes sont fatigués, sans parler des enfants.</i><br><br>• 晴天还迷路，何况在雾里。<br><small><span class=""t2"">qíng</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span> <span class=""t2"">mí</span><span class=""t4"">lù</span>，<span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t4"">kuàng</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t4"">wù</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span>。</small><br><i>Si on se perd même par beau temps, sans parler dans le brouillard.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">非…不可 — il faut absolument / on doit forcément</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	<b>Carte en double.</b><br><br>Le même point est traité par la fiche « 非…不可 — il faut absolument / impossible de ne pas    HSK 4 ». Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">反而 — au contraire / à l'inverse</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>反而</b> introduit un résultat contraire à ce qu'on attendrait. Souvent après 不但…, 没想到, 结果…<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 不但没减肥，反而胖了。<br><small><span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t3"">jiǎn</span><span class=""t2"">féi</span>， <span class=""t3"">fǎn</span><span class=""t2"">ér</span> <span class=""t4"">pàng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Non seulement il n'a pas maigri, mais il a grossi.</i><br><br>• 他越解释，大家反而越不信。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">yuè</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span>， <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t3"">fǎn</span><span class=""t2"">ér</span> <span class=""t4"">yuè</span><span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">xìn</span>。</small><br><i>Plus il s'explique, plus les gens ne le croient pas.</i><br><br>• 休息了一天，反而更累了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">xiū</span><span class=""t0"">xi</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">tiān</span>， <span class=""t3"">fǎn</span><span class=""t2"">ér</span> <span class=""t4"">gèng</span><span class=""t4"">lèi</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Après une journée de repos, il est encore plus fatigué.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">即便/即使 vs 就算/哪怕 — même si (concession)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Tous signifient ""même si"" mais avec des nuances de registre :<br><b>即使/即便</b> = registre écrit. <b>就算</b> = oral courant. <b>哪怕</b> = émotionnel/dramatique.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 即便失败，我也不后悔。<br><small><span class=""t2"">jí</span><span class=""t4"">biàn</span> <span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">hòu</span><span class=""t3"">huǐ</span>。</small><br><i>Même en échouant, je ne regrette pas.</i><br><br>• 就算再难，你也得坚持。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">suàn</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t2"">nán</span>， <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t3"">děi</span> <span class=""t1"">jiān</span><span class=""t2"">chí</span>。</small><br><i>Aussi difficile que ce soit, tu dois tenir.</i><br><br>• 哪怕只剩一口气，也要说出真相。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǎ</span><span class=""t4"">pà</span> <span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">shèng</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t3"">kǒu</span><span class=""t4"">qì</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span><span class=""t1"">chū</span> <span class=""t1"">zhēn</span><span class=""t4"">xiàng</span>。</small><br><i>Même s'il ne me reste qu'un souffle, je dirai la vérité.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">对于 vs 关于 — concernant / à propos de</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>关于</b> = sur le sujet de (thème, titre). <b>对于</b> = par rapport à / envers (point de vue ou attitude). 关于 + nom se place avant le sujet (ou sert de titre) ; 对于 + nom peut se placer avant ou après le sujet.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 关于这个问题，我有几点看法。<br><small><span class=""t1"">guān</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">jǐ</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">kàn</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span>。</small><br><i>Sur ce sujet, j'ai quelques opinions.</i><br><br>• 对于这个问题，他持保留态度。<br><small><span class=""t4"">duì</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span><span class=""t2"">chí</span> <span class=""t3"">bǎo</span><span class=""t2"">liú</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t4"">dù</span>。</small><br><i>Par rapport à ce problème, il adopte une attitude réservée.</i><br><br>• 关于这件事，你怎么看？<br><small><span class=""t1"">guān</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span>， <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">zěn</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t4"">kàn</span>？</small><br><i>Sur cette affaire, qu'en penses-tu ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">未免 vs 不免 — quelque peu (trop) / inévitablement</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>未免</b> = quelque peu (critique atténuée : c'est un peu excessif). <b>不免</b> = inévitablement (résultat difficile à éviter).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 你这样做未免太草率了。<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">yàng</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t4"">wèi</span><span class=""t3"">miǎn</span><span class=""t4"">tài</span> <span class=""t3"">cǎo</span><span class=""t4"">shuài</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Agir ainsi est quelque peu imprudent.</i><br><br>• 听到这个消息，大家不免有些担心。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tīng</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t1"">xiāo</span><span class=""t0"">xi</span>， <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">miǎn</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t1"">xiē</span> <span class=""t1"">dān</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span>。</small><br><i>En apprenant cette nouvelle, tout le monde ne pouvait s'empêcher de s'inquiéter.</i><br><br>• 这个价格未免太贵了吧。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">jià</span><span class=""t2"">gé</span> <span class=""t4"">wèi</span><span class=""t3"">miǎn</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t4"">guì</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t0"">ba</span>。</small><br><i>Ce prix est quand même un peu trop cher.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2 corrige_2026	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">未免 vs 不免 — inévitablement / quelque peu</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	<b>Carte remplacée.</b><br><br>Une faute dans l'énoncé a été corrigée : la version juste est une nouvelle carte du même paquet. Celle-ci peut être supprimée (dans le navigateur d'Anki : <i>tag:a_supprimer</i>).		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2 a_supprimer	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">是否 — si / est-ce que (soutenu)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>是否</b> = si / est-ce que. Forme écrite/soutenue de 是不是 ou 有没有. Souvent dans des textes officiels ou des questions indirectes.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 请确认是否收到邮件。<br><small><span class=""t3"">qǐng</span> <span class=""t4"">què</span><span class=""t4"">rèn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t3"">fǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">shōu</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t2"">yóu</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span>。</small><br><i>Veuillez confirmer si vous avez bien reçu l'email.</i><br><br>• 我不知道他是否愿意参加。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t0"">dao</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t3"">fǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">yuàn</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t1"">cān</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span>。</small><br><i>Je ne sais pas s'il voudra participer.</i><br><br>• 请告知是否有任何问题。<br><small><span class=""t3"">qǐng</span> <span class=""t4"">gào</span><span class=""t1"">zhī</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t3"">fǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">rèn</span><span class=""t2"">hé</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>。</small><br><i>Veuillez nous indiquer si vous avez la moindre question.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">所 + V — ce qui est V (nominalisation formelle)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>所</b> + verbe transitif = forme nominale soutenue (ce qui est V). 所知 = ce qu'on sait. 所见 = ce qu'on voit. 所有 = tout ce qu'on possède.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 如你所知，这件事已经解决了。<br><small><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t1"">zhī</span>， <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t1"">jīng</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Comme tu le sais, cette affaire est réglée.</i><br><br>• 他把所有的积蓄都投了进去。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">bǎ</span> <span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">jī</span><span class=""t4"">xù</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">tóu</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t4"">jìn</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span>。</small><br><i>Il a investi toutes ses économies.</i><br><br>• 所见即所得。<br><small><span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t2"">jí</span> <span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span>。</small><br><i>Ce qu'on voit est ce qu'on obtient (WYSIWYG).</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">与其说…不如说… — plutôt que de dire A, mieux vaut dire B</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Rectification d'une caractérisation : A est partiellement vrai mais B est plus juste.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 与其说他懒，不如说他没有找到动力。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">lǎn</span>， <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t3"">zhǎo</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">dòng</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt que de dire qu'il est paresseux, il vaut mieux dire qu'il n'a pas trouvé sa motivation.</i><br><br>• 这与其说是问题，不如说是机会。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span>， <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">jī</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt que d'appeler ça un problème, mieux vaut y voir une opportunité.</i><br><br>• 与其说她成功，不如说她幸运。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span>， <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">xìng</span><span class=""t4"">yùn</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt que de dire qu'elle a réussi, il vaut mieux dire qu'elle a eu de la chance.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">莫不如 / 倒不如 — autant / il vaudrait mieux</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>倒不如</b> / <b>莫不如</b> = il vaudrait mieux / autant (suggestion alternative plus judicieuse). Registre oral/neutre.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 与其在这里等，倒不如先去吃饭。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t3"">lǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">děng</span>， <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t1"">xiān</span> <span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t1"">chī</span><span class=""t4"">fàn</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt que d'attendre ici, autant aller manger d'abord.</i><br><br>• 这样做太麻烦了，莫不如换个方法。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">yàng</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span> <span class=""t2"">má</span><span class=""t0"">fan</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t4"">mò</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t4"">huàn</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span>。</small><br><i>C'est trop compliqué comme ça, autant changer de méthode.</i><br><br>• 与其犯愁，倒不如想想解决办法。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span> <span class=""t4"">fàn</span><span class=""t2"">chóu</span>， <span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span> <span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span><span class=""t0"">xiang</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span><span class=""t4"">bàn</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt que de se morfondre, autant réfléchir à des solutions.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">宁肯…也要 — plutôt…même si (volonté forte)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>宁肯</b> + sacrifice + <b>也要</b> + but. Exprime la détermination à atteindre un but malgré le prix à payer. Plus résolu que 宁可…也不 (refus).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 宁肯不睡觉，也要把这道题做出来。<br><small><span class=""t4"">nìng</span><span class=""t3"">kěn</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span> <span class=""t4"">shuì</span><span class=""t4"">jiào</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t3"">bǎ</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t1"">chū</span><span class=""t2"">lái</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt ne pas dormir que de ne pas résoudre ce problème.</i><br><br>• 她宁肯少挣钱，也要做自己喜欢的工作。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">nìng</span><span class=""t3"">kěn</span> <span class=""t3"">shǎo</span> <span class=""t4"">zhèng</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t4"">zì</span><span class=""t3"">jǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">xǐ</span><span class=""t0"">huan</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span>。</small><br><i>Elle préfère gagner moins pour faire un travail qu'elle aime.</i><br><br>• 宁肯被人误解，也要说出真相。<br><small><span class=""t4"">nìng</span><span class=""t3"">kěn</span> <span class=""t4"">bèi</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t4"">wù</span><span class=""t3"">jiě</span>， <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span><span class=""t1"">chū</span> <span class=""t1"">zhēn</span><span class=""t4"">xiàng</span>。</small><br><i>Plutôt être mal compris que de ne pas dire la vérité.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">不禁 — ne pas pouvoir s'empêcher de</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>不禁</b> + V = ne pas pouvoir s'empêcher de faire V (réaction spontanée). Registre soigné.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 听到这个消息，她不禁流下了眼泪。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tīng</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t1"">xiāo</span><span class=""t0"">xi</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t1"">jīn</span> <span class=""t2"">liú</span><span class=""t4"">xià</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t3"">yǎn</span><span class=""t4"">lèi</span>。</small><br><i>En entendant cette nouvelle, elle ne put s'empêcher de verser des larmes.</i><br><br>• 这个故事太有趣了，让人不禁会心一笑。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t4"">gù</span><span class=""t0"">shi</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t4"">qù</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>， <span class=""t4"">ràng</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t1"">jīn</span> <span class=""t4"">huì</span><span class=""t1"">xīn</span><span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">xiào</span>。</small><br><i>Cette histoire est si amusante qu'elle vous arrache un sourire complice.</i><br><br>• 看着眼前的美景，他不禁感叹大自然的神奇。<br><small><span class=""t4"">kàn</span><span class=""t0"">zhe</span> <span class=""t3"">yǎn</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">měi</span><span class=""t3"">jǐng</span>， <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t1"">jīn</span> <span class=""t3"">gǎn</span><span class=""t4"">tàn</span> <span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t4"">zì</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">shén</span><span class=""t2"">qí</span>。</small><br><i>Devant ce magnifique paysage, il ne put s'empêcher d'admirer la magie de la nature.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">何尝 — ne pas du tout (rhétorique négatif)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>何尝</b> + 不/没 = est-ce qu'on n'a pas / n'a-t-on jamais. Question rhétorique affirmant le contraire.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他何尝不想回家？只是没有机会。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t2"">cháng</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t2"">huí</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span>？ <span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t1"">jī</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span>。</small><br><i>Croit-on vraiment qu'il ne veuille pas rentrer ? Il n'en a simplement pas eu l'occasion.</i><br><br>• 我何尝不理解你的苦衷。<br><small><span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t2"">cháng</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐ</span><span class=""t3"">jiě</span> <span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">kǔ</span><span class=""t1"">zhōng</span>。</small><br><i>Je comprends parfaitement tes difficultés.</i><br><br>• 谁何尝没有过这样的时刻呢？<br><small><span class=""t2"">shuí</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t2"">cháng</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">guò</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">yàng</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t4"">kè</span> <span class=""t0"">ne</span>？</small><br><i>Qui parmi nous n'a pas vécu de tels moments ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">诚然 — certes / il est vrai que (formel)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>诚然</b> = certes / il est vrai que (concession solennelle). Registre écrit soutenu. Suivi souvent d'une nuance introduite par 但是/然而.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 诚然，这个方法有一定的局限性，但总体上利大于弊。<br><small><span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span>， <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t3"">fǎ</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">dìng</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">jú</span><span class=""t4"">xiàn</span><span class=""t4"">xìng</span>， <span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t3"">zǒng</span><span class=""t3"">tǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">shàng</span> <span class=""t4"">lì</span><span class=""t4"">dà</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span><span class=""t4"">bì</span>。</small><br><i>Certes, cette méthode a certaines limites, mais dans l'ensemble les avantages l'emportent.</i><br><br>• 诚然，失败是痛苦的，但它也是成长的必经之路。<br><small><span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span>， <span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">tòng</span><span class=""t3"">kǔ</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span>， <span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t3"">zhǎng</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">bì</span><span class=""t1"">jīng</span><span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t4"">lù</span>。</small><br><i>Il est vrai que l'échec est douloureux, mais c'est aussi un passage obligé de la croissance.</i><br><br>• 诚然如此，但问题并不是那么简单。<br><small><span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t2"">rán</span><span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span>， <span class=""t4"">dàn</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">nà</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t3"">jiǎn</span><span class=""t1"">dān</span>。</small><br><i>Certes, mais le problème n'est pas aussi simple que ça.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">何止 — bien plus que / pas seulement</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>何止</b> + X = bien plus que X (renforcement). Souvent suivi d'un chiffre ou d'une évaluation.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这部电影何止好看，简直是杰作！<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t4"">diàn</span><span class=""t3"">yǐng</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span><span class=""t4"">kàn</span>， <span class=""t3"">jiǎn</span><span class=""t2"">zhí</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">jié</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span>！</small><br><i>Ce film va bien au-delà d'être bon — c'est carrément un chef-d'œuvre !</i><br><br>• 他欠的债何止十万。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">qiàn</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">zhài</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">shí</span><span class=""t4"">wàn</span>。</small><br><i>Sa dette dépasse de loin cent mille yuans.</i><br><br>• 受益的何止是她一个人。<br><small><span class=""t4"">shòu</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t2"">yí</span><span class=""t4"">gè</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span>。</small><br><i>Ce n'est pas seulement elle qui en a bénéficié.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">并非 — n'est absolument pas (formel)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>并非</b> = absolument pas / loin d'être. Négation forte, registre écrit. Plus emphatique que 不是.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他并非不努力，只是方向错了。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span><span class=""t1"">fēi</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span>， <span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">fāng</span><span class=""t4"">xiàng</span> <span class=""t4"">cuò</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>。</small><br><i>Il est loin d'être paresseux — c'est juste que sa direction est mauvaise.</i><br><br>• 问题的解决并非一朝一夕之事。<br><small><span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t2"">jué</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span><span class=""t1"">fēi</span> <span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">zhāo</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span><span class=""t1"">xī</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span>。</small><br><i>La résolution du problème n'est absolument pas une affaire d'un jour.</i><br><br>• 成功并非终点，失败也并非末日。<br><small><span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span><span class=""t1"">fēi</span> <span class=""t1"">zhōng</span><span class=""t3"">diǎn</span>， <span class=""t1"">shī</span><span class=""t4"">bài</span> <span class=""t3"">yě</span> <span class=""t4"">bìng</span><span class=""t1"">fēi</span> <span class=""t4"">mò</span><span class=""t4"">rì</span>。</small><br><i>Le succès n'est absolument pas une fin, l'échec n'est pas non plus une catastrophe.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">堪称 — mériter d'être appelé / digne d'être dit</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>堪称</b> + titre/qualification = mériter d'être qualifié de, être digne d'être dit. Registre soutenu/journalistique.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这部作品堪称当代文学的里程碑。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t4"">zuò</span><span class=""t3"">pǐn</span> <span class=""t1"">kān</span><span class=""t1"">chēng</span> <span class=""t1"">dāng</span><span class=""t4"">dài</span><span class=""t2"">wén</span><span class=""t2"">xué</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐ</span><span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">bēi</span>。</small><br><i>Cette œuvre mérite d'être qualifiée de jalon de la littérature contemporaine.</i><br><br>• 他的操作堪称教科书级别。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">cāo</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t1"">kān</span><span class=""t1"">chēng</span> <span class=""t4"">jiào</span><span class=""t1"">kē</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span> <span class=""t2"">jí</span><span class=""t2"">bié</span>。</small><br><i>Son exécution mérite d'être qualifiée de niveau manuel scolaire.</i><br><br>• 这场演出堪称完美。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t3"">chǎng</span> <span class=""t3"">yǎn</span><span class=""t1"">chū</span> <span class=""t1"">kān</span><span class=""t1"">chēng</span> <span class=""t2"">wán</span><span class=""t3"">měi</span>。</small><br><i>Cette performance mérite d'être qualifiée de parfaite.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">鉴于 — étant donné que / en raison de (formel)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>鉴于</b> + fait/situation = étant donné que / compte tenu de. Registre formel/administratif.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 鉴于目前的情况，会议决定延期举行。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t4"">mù</span><span class=""t2"">qián</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">qíng</span><span class=""t4"">kuàng</span>， <span class=""t4"">huì</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t2"">jué</span><span class=""t4"">dìng</span> <span class=""t2"">yán</span><span class=""t1"">qī</span> <span class=""t3"">jǔ</span><span class=""t2"">xíng</span>。</small><br><i>Étant donné la situation actuelle, il a été décidé de reporter la réunion.</i><br><br>• 鉴于您的特殊贡献，特此颁发荣誉证书。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t2"">nín</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">tè</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span> <span class=""t4"">gòng</span><span class=""t4"">xiàn</span>， <span class=""t4"">tè</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">bān</span><span class=""t1"">fā</span> <span class=""t2"">róng</span><span class=""t4"">yù</span><span class=""t4"">zhèng</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span>。</small><br><i>En raison de votre contribution particulière, nous décernons par la présente un certificat d'honneur.</i><br><br>• 鉴于以上分析，我们建议采取以下措施。<br><small><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t2"">yú</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t4"">shàng</span> <span class=""t1"">fēn</span><span class=""t1"">xī</span>， <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span><span class=""t0"">men</span> <span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t4"">yì</span> <span class=""t3"">cǎi</span><span class=""t3"">qǔ</span> <span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t4"">xià</span> <span class=""t4"">cuò</span><span class=""t1"">shī</span>。</small><br><i>À la lumière de l'analyse ci-dessus, nous recommandons de prendre les mesures suivantes.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">乃至 / 甚至 — et même / jusqu'à</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5-6</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>甚至 = même / jusqu'à (courant). 乃至 = et même / voire (registre écrit soutenu). Mettent en relief l'exemple le plus extrême d'une liste.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 他会说三门语言，甚至还会写毛笔字。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span> <span class=""t1"">sān</span><span class=""t2"">mén</span> <span class=""t3"">yǔ</span><span class=""t2"">yán</span>， <span class=""t4"">shèn</span><span class=""t4"">zhì</span> <span class=""t2"">hái</span><span class=""t4"">huì</span> <span class=""t3"">xiě</span><span class=""t2"">máo</span><span class=""t3"">bǐ</span><span class=""t4"">zì</span>。</small><br><i>Il parle trois langues, et sait même écrire la calligraphie.</i><br><br>• 影响了全国乃至世界的发展。<br><small><span class=""t3"">yǐng</span><span class=""t3"">xiǎng</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span> <span class=""t2"">quán</span><span class=""t2"">guó</span> <span class=""t3"">nǎi</span><span class=""t4"">zhì</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t4"">jiè</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">fā</span><span class=""t3"">zhǎn</span>。</small><br><i>A influencé le développement du pays entier, voire du monde.</i><br><br>• 甚至连专家都搞不清楚。<br><small><span class=""t4"">shèn</span><span class=""t4"">zhì</span> <span class=""t2"">lián</span> <span class=""t1"">zhuān</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t3"">gǎo</span><span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t1"">qīng</span><span class=""t3"">chǔ</span>。</small><br><i>Même les experts n'y comprennent rien.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">倒是 — certes / justement (avec nuance)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5-6</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>倒是</b> sert à concéder partiellement tout en introduisant une réserve ou une ironie. Peut marquer la surprise, la concession ou la contradiction avec les attentes.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 这件衣服倒是不贵，就是颜色不好看。<br><small><span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t1"">yī</span><span class=""t0"">fu</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">guì</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">yán</span><span class=""t4"">sè</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span> <span class=""t3"">hǎo</span><span class=""t4"">kàn</span>。</small><br><i>Ce vêtement n'est certes pas cher, mais la couleur n'est pas belle.</i><br><br>• 他工作倒是认真，就是态度有些傲慢。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">gōng</span><span class=""t4"">zuò</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">rèn</span><span class=""t1"">zhēn</span>， <span class=""t4"">jiù</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t4"">tài</span><span class=""t4"">dù</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t1"">xiē</span> <span class=""t4"">ào</span><span class=""t4"">màn</span>。</small><br><i>Son travail est certes sérieux, mais son attitude est un peu arrogante.</i><br><br>• 你倒是提醒我了！<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">tí</span><span class=""t3"">xǐng</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t0"">le</span>！</small><br><i>Tiens, tu me rappelles quelque chose !</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">之所以 / 所以 / 因为 — explicatifs comparés</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5-6</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>因为…所以</b> = parce que…donc (cause → conséquence, oral/écrit). <b>之所以…是因为</b> = si…c'est parce que (résultat → cause expliquée, écrit soutenu).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 因为下雨，所以我没出门。<br><small><span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t4"">xià</span><span class=""t3"">yǔ</span>， <span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">wǒ</span> <span class=""t2"">méi</span> <span class=""t1"">chū</span><span class=""t2"">mén</span>。</small><br><i>Parce qu'il pleuvait, je ne suis pas sorti.</i><br><br>• 他之所以成功，是因为坚持不懈。<br><small><span class=""t1"">tā</span> <span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">chéng</span><span class=""t1"">gōng</span>， <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t1"">jiān</span><span class=""t2"">chí</span><span class=""t2"">bú</span><span class=""t4"">xiè</span>。</small><br><i>S'il réussit, c'est parce qu'il persévère sans relâche.</i><br><br>• 之所以这么说，是因为有事实依据。<br><small><span class=""t1"">zhī</span><span class=""t3"">suǒ</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span><span class=""t0"">me</span> <span class=""t1"">shuō</span>， <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t1"">yīn</span><span class=""t4"">wèi</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">shì</span><span class=""t2"">shí</span> <span class=""t1"">yī</span><span class=""t4"">jù</span>。</small><br><i>Si je dis cela, c'est parce qu'il y a des faits à l'appui.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">凡是 / 只要 — tout ce qui / dès que (généralisation)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 5-6</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>凡是</b> = tout ce qui / quiconque (généralisation universelle, souvent avec 都). <b>只要</b> = du moment que (condition suffisante).<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 凡是参加的人，都能领到纪念品。<br><small><span class=""t2"">fán</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t1"">cān</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span>， <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t3"">lǐng</span><span class=""t4"">dào</span> <span class=""t4"">jì</span><span class=""t4"">niàn</span><span class=""t3"">pǐn</span>。</small><br><i>Tous ceux qui participent peuvent recevoir un souvenir.</i><br><br>• 凡是读过这本书的人，都深受感动。<br><small><span class=""t2"">fán</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">dú</span><span class=""t4"">guò</span> <span class=""t4"">zhè</span> <span class=""t3"">běn</span><span class=""t1"">shū</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">rén</span>， <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t1"">shēn</span><span class=""t4"">shòu</span><span class=""t3"">gǎn</span><span class=""t4"">dòng</span>。</small><br><i>Tous ceux qui ont lu ce livre en ont été profondément touchés.</i><br><br>• 只要有网，凡是能在线的都可以参加。<br><small><span class=""t3"">zhǐ</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span> <span class=""t3"">yǒu</span><span class=""t3"">wǎng</span>， <span class=""t2"">fán</span><span class=""t4"">shì</span> <span class=""t2"">néng</span> <span class=""t4"">zài</span><span class=""t4"">xiàn</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t1"">dōu</span> <span class=""t3"">kě</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t1"">cān</span><span class=""t1"">jiā</span>。</small><br><i>Du moment qu'il y a internet, quiconque peut être en ligne peut participer.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::5 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::5 · HSK 5
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">可见 / 由此可见 — on voit ainsi / il en résulte que</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 6</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br>Connecteur logique de conclusion. Introduit une déduction à partir de ce qui précède. Registre écrit/académique.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 数据如此，可见问题的严重性。<br><small><span class=""t4"">shù</span><span class=""t4"">jù</span> <span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span>， <span class=""t3"">kě</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span> <span class=""t4"">wèn</span><span class=""t2"">tí</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t2"">yán</span><span class=""t4"">zhòng</span><span class=""t4"">xìng</span>。</small><br><i>Devant ces données, on voit la gravité du problème.</i><br><br>• 由此可见，努力的重要性不言而喻。<br><small><span class=""t2"">yóu</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span><span class=""t3"">kě</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span>， <span class=""t3"">nǔ</span><span class=""t4"">lì</span> <span class=""t0"">de</span> <span class=""t4"">zhòng</span><span class=""t4"">yào</span><span class=""t4"">xìng</span> <span class=""t4"">bù</span><span class=""t2"">yán</span><span class=""t2"">ér</span><span class=""t4"">yù</span>。</small><br><i>Il en résulte que l'importance de l'effort va de soi.</i><br><br>• 由此可见，两者密切相关。<br><small><span class=""t2"">yóu</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span><span class=""t3"">kě</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span>， <span class=""t3"">liǎng</span><span class=""t3"">zhě</span> <span class=""t4"">mì</span><span class=""t4"">qiè</span><span class=""t1"">xiāng</span><span class=""t1"">guān</span>。</small><br><i>On voit ainsi que les deux sont étroitement liés.</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::6 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::6 · HSK 6
"<b>Structure :</b><br><br><span style=""font-size:130%;color:#2980b9"">何以 — comment / pourquoi (littéraire/rhétorique)</span><br><br><small style=""color:#888"">HSK 6</small>"	"<div class=""exemple-bloc"" style=""text-align:left""><b>Explication :</b><br><b>何以</b> = comment / par quel moyen / pourquoi. Registre classique/littéraire. Correspond à 怎么 / 为什么 dans le registre formel.<br><br><b>Exemples :</b><br>• 何以解忧？唯有杜康。<br><small><span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t3"">jiě</span><span class=""t1"">yōu</span>？ <span class=""t2"">wéi</span><span class=""t3"">yǒu</span> <span class=""t4"">dù</span><span class=""t1"">kāng</span>。</small><br><i>Comment dissiper ses soucis ? Par le vin seul. (Cao Cao)</i><br><br>• 何以见得？<br><small><span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span><span class=""t4"">jiàn</span><span class=""t2"">dé</span>？</small><br><i>Qu'est-ce qui te fait dire cela ? (litt. « à quoi le voit-on ? »)</i><br><br>• 你何以如此确定？<br><small><span class=""t3"">nǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">hé</span><span class=""t3"">yǐ</span> <span class=""t2"">rú</span><span class=""t3"">cǐ</span> <span class=""t4"">què</span><span class=""t4"">dìng</span>？</small><br><i>Comment peux-tu en être si certain ?</i><br><br></div>"		HSK::6 grammaire deck_v2	Chinois::Grammaire::6 · HSK 6
